+# AUTH rules — login/logout/session/access — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c (frozen ~/app)
+
+Scope: `LoginServlet`, `LogoutServlet`, `AuthFilter`, `index.jsp`, `web.xml`. Paths relative to `src/main/java/com/acme/tracker/` unless noted. Current observed behavior; no policy inferred from role names. Approve/reject gating is NOT duplicated here — it is R-APR-001 (kept stable).
+
+## R-AUTH-001 — GET /login renders the sign-in form
+- **ID:** R-AUTH-001
+- **Statement:** GET /login always renders the login form (200 HTML), whether or not a session/user already exists.
+- **Source citations:** `web/LoginServlet.java:16-20` (doGet forwards to `/WEB-INF/jsp/login.jsp`); exemption `web/AuthFilter.java:28-31`; mapping `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/web.xml:26-33`; form/error markup `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/login.jsp:18-33`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any client (anonymous or authenticated); `/login` is exempt from AuthFilter.
+- **Trigger:** `GET /login`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200, login form; no error paragraph unless `error` attribute set (login.jsp:20). No session created on GET.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed only; no disagreement known.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-AUTH-001.
+
+## R-AUTH-002 — POST /login success binds session user and redirects to /tasks
+- **ID:** R-AUTH-002
+- **Statement:** POST /login with a username+password matching a `person` row stores that Person as session attribute `user` and responds 302 to `<ctx>/tasks`.
+- **Source citations:** `web/LoginServlet.java:23-35` (`authenticate` at `:27`, `req.getSession(true).setAttribute("user", user)` `:33`, `sendRedirect(ctx + "/tasks")` `:34`); SQL `dao/PersonDao.java:16-29` (`WHERE username = ? AND password = ?` — plaintext comparison, comment `:17`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any client; credentials must match a seeded row exactly (seed: `src/main/resources/db/seed.sql:2-6`, password = username).
+- **Trigger:** `POST /login` with valid `username`/`password` form params.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `Location: /tasks`; session cookie issued (JSESSIONID); session attribute `user` set. A second login in the same session replaces the bound user (no rotation; `:33` reuses the existing session from `getSession(true)`).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed: passwords stored/compared in plaintext; session not invalidated before re-login (fixation-rotation absent). Recorded as observations for the orchestrator, not adjudicated here.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-AUTH-002.
+
+## R-AUTH-003 — POST /login failure re-renders form with "Invalid credentials" (200, not 401)
+- **ID:** R-AUTH-003
+- **Statement:** POST /login with wrong, missing, or null username/password yields 200 re-render of the login page with error text "Invalid credentials"; no session is created.
+- **Source citations:** `web/LoginServlet.java:28-32` (user == null → `setAttribute("error","Invalid credentials")` + forward); `dao/PersonDao.java:18-24` (SQL `username = ? AND password = ?`; NULL params can never match — SQL `= NULL` is not true, and both columns are NOT NULL per `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql:11,13`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any client.
+- **Trigger:** `POST /login` with non-matching credentials (includes absent params, empty strings, unknown user).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200 with `<p class="error">Invalid credentials</p>` (login.jsp:20); no session cookie issued on this path; no redirect.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed failure = 200, not 401/403. No disagreement known; characterize as-is.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-AUTH-003.
+
+## R-AUTH-004 — AuthFilter exemptions: exact /login, prefix /api/, suffix .css
+- **ID:** R-AUTH-004
+- **Statement:** Requests whose servlet path equals `/login`, starts with `/api/`, or ends with `.css` bypass authentication entirely; all other paths require a session user.
+- **Source citations:** `web/AuthFilter.java:28-31`; filter mapped `/*` by `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/web.xml:16-23`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any request reaching the filter.
+- **Trigger:** any request; path test at `:28`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** Exempt → chain continues unauthenticated. Note (observed): the `.css` suffix exemption is path-suffix-based, so a hypothetical route ending in `.css` would be unauthenticated — no such mapped route exists today (`/tasks/action.css` etc. fall through to the default servlet → 404). `/api/tasks` exemption = closed finding F-003 (not a defect; GET-only read-back).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; F-003 records the /api/* adjudication.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-AUTH-004 (partial: exemption leg), S-API-001.
+
+## R-AUTH-005 — AuthFilter protects every non-exempt path (302 to /login when no session user)
+- **ID:** R-AUTH-005
+- **Statement:** For any path not exempt under R-AUTH-004, a request without session attribute `user` is redirected 302 to `<ctx>/login` and never reaches a servlet; an authenticated request continues.
+- **Source citations:** `web/AuthFilter.java:33-38` (`getSession(false)`, user null → `sendRedirect(ctx + "/login")`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** anonymous client (no JSESSIONID or expired/invalidated session).
+- **Trigger:** GET/POST to `/tasks`, `/tasks/form`, `/tasks/action`, `/projects`, `/projects/delete` (unmapped → 404 after auth), `/logout`, `/index.jsp`, or any other non-exempt path.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `Location: /login`; no DAO access, no DB write. Same behavior for GET and POST.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; matches inherited S-APR-005 evidence (approve/reject leg, run 01).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-AUTH-004, S-AUTH-005.
+
+## R-AUTH-006 — GET /logout invalidates the session and redirects to /login
+- **ID:** R-AUTH-006
+- **Statement:** GET /logout invalidates the current session (if any) and responds 302 to `<ctx>/login`; the old session cookie is dead afterwards.
+- **Source citations:** `web/LogoutServlet.java:12-18` (`getSession(false)`; invalidate if non-null; `sendRedirect(ctx + "/login")`); mapping `web.xml:35-42`; logout link `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/_header.jspf:15`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any client; /logout is NOT exempt (R-AUTH-004), so an anonymous GET /logout is first redirected to /login by the filter (R-AUTH-005) — the servlet's own redirect is only observable when authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `GET /logout` with a live session.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/login`; session invalidated — replaying the old JSESSIONID on `/tasks` now yields 302 `/login` (R-AUTH-005). No DB write.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; no disagreement known.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-AUTH-006.
+
+## R-AUTH-007 — /logout is GET-only; other methods get 405
+- **ID:** R-AUTH-007
+- **Statement:** Only doGet is implemented on LogoutServlet; POST (and other non-HEAD methods) fall through to HttpServlet's default and respond 405.
+- **Source citations:** `web/LogoutServlet.java:12-18` (doGet only; no doPost override); javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet default doPost → 405 (container behavior, no app source).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated client (passes filter).
+- **Trigger:** `POST /logout` (also PUT/DELETE).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 405, no session invalidation (observable: pre-logout session still works on /tasks).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed container default; flagged so tests don't assume 302.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-AUTH-006 (companion refusal leg).
+
+## R-AUTH-008 — Root/index redirect behavior (welcome-file dispatch uncertainty)
+- **ID:** R-AUTH-008
+- **Statement:** `index.jsp` unconditionally sends 302 to `<ctx>/tasks` (src/main/webapp/index.jsp:1). Direct `GET /index.jsp` passes AuthFilter (path `/index.jsp` is not exempt) → anonymous 302 `/login`, authenticated 302 `/tasks`. For the bare `/` welcome-file request, whether AuthFilter runs before index.jsp depends on container welcome-file dispatch semantics and is NOT decidable from app source alone.
+- **Source citations:** `src/main/webapp/index.jsp:1`; welcome-file `web.xml:89-91`; filter mapping `web.xml:20-23`; exemption list `web/AuthFilter.java:28-31`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any client; ROOT context assumed (ctx = "").
+- **Trigger:** `GET /` or `GET /index.jsp`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** Two candidate outcomes for `GET /` anonymous: (a) filter sees the welcome-file target → 302 `/login` immediately, or (b) welcome-file forward bypasses REQUEST-dispatcher filters → index.jsp's own 302 `/tasks`, after which the browser's /tasks request hits the filter → 302 `/login`. Either way an anonymous user ends at /login; the immediate `Location` for `GET /` differs (`/login` vs `/tasks`). `GET /index.jsp` is deterministic: anonymous → 302 `/login`; authenticated → 302 `/tasks`.
+- **Observed versus intended:** UNRESOLVED observed detail — single probe needed (recipe in evidence/analyst/d002-run02-notes.md §AUTH-008). Intended is uncontroversial (end at login/tasks); only the first hop is in question.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-AUTH-007.
+
+## Corrections to inherited material (explicit, nothing removed)
+- None required in R-APR/S-APR for AUTH: R-APR-001's filter citation (`AuthFilter.java:40-47`) remains the approve/reject gate; this file does not supersede it.
+- Note: run-summary/01 measured "17 classes" in coverage while `_campaign/scope.json` lists 18 Java sources — a run-01 measurement discrepancy recorded for the orchestrator in evidence/analyst/d002-run02-notes.md; does not affect AUTH rules.

+# BULK rules — bulkClose — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c (frozen ~/app)
+
+Scope: `TaskActionServlet.handleBulkClose`, `TaskDao.bulkClose`, tasks.jsp bulk form. Inherited F-001 (suspected defect) is characterized here explicitly, with the intended requirement recorded separately as unresolved. Paths relative to `src/main/java/com/acme/tracker/`.
+
+## R-BULK-001 — Empty selection (no ids param) → 302 no-op
+- **ID:** R-BULK-001
+- **Statement:** POST /tasks/action action=bulkClose with no `ids` parameter values responds 302 to /tasks and changes nothing.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:77-81` (`getParameterValues("ids")` null or length 0 → redirect); bulk form only submits checked boxes (`src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/tasks.jsp:47` — unchecked boxes are not sent).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user.
+- **Trigger:** `POST /tasks/action` `action=bulkClose` (no ids) — the natural "no checkbox checked" case.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/tasks`; oracle unchanged. Note: `ids=` (param present with a single empty-string value) is NOT this case — it parses "" → NFE → 500 (R-BULK-004).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-BULK-001.
+
+## R-BULK-002 — Selected non-DONE ids → DONE with updated_at bump; DONE ids untouched; unknown ids ignored (characterization-of-suspect)
+- **ID:** R-BULK-002
+- **Statement:** With ids selected, every id whose row exists and is not already DONE is set to DONE with updated_at bumped, regardless of its current status (OPEN/IN_PROGRESS/PENDING_APPROVAL) — in a single UPDATE; already-DONE rows are not touched (no bump); nonexistent ids are silently ignored; the response is always 302 /tasks.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:82-87` (parse loop, `tasks.bulkClose(ids)`, redirect); `dao/TaskDao.java:128-147` (`UPDATE task SET status='DONE', updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE status <> 'DONE' AND id IN (...)`, return value ignored).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user; mixed selection allowed.
+- **Trigger:** `action=bulkClose`, `ids=1&ids=3` (OPEN + PENDING_APPROVAL), or `ids=4&ids=999` (DONE + unknown).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/tasks`; oracle: non-DONE selected → DONE (+updated_at), DONE selected unchanged, unknown ids no error.
+- **Observed versus intended:** **OBSERVED = characterization-of-suspect (F-001):** a PENDING_APPROVAL task reaches DONE without approval. INTENDED (recorded separately, UNRESOLVED — needs product decision, do not test as requirement): under the approval policy of R-APR-001/R-APR-004 only ADMIN/MANAGER and only PENDING_APPROVAL tasks should reach DONE. See R-BULK-003.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-BULK-002 (characterization-of-suspect).
+
+## R-BULK-003 — No canApprove gate and no PENDING_APPROVAL guard on bulkClose (characterization-of-suspect; intended unresolved)
+- **ID:** R-BULK-003
+- **Statement:** The bulkClose path performs no role check (the AuthFilter gate covers only approve/reject — `web/AuthFilter.java:42`) and no status guard other than `status <> 'DONE'`; therefore MEMBER and VIEWER users can drive any task to DONE in bulk, including PENDING_APPROVAL tasks.
+- **Source citations:** `web/AuthFilter.java:40-47` (gate limited to approve/reject strings); `web/TaskActionServlet.java:26-29` (bulkClose branches BEFORE the id/404 logic and before requireApprover — no gate on this path); `dao/TaskDao.java:136-137` (only `status <> 'DONE'`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user, any roles, any task states.
+- **Trigger:** login dev/dev; `POST /tasks/action` `action=bulkClose`, `ids=3` (PENDING_APPROVAL).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/tasks`; task 3 DONE (if the suspected behavior is real — reproduction pending, recipe in findings/ledger.json F-001).
+- **Observed versus intended:** **OBSERVED (from source) = characterization-of-suspect, F-001.** INTENDED, recorded separately and explicitly NOT speced as a requirement: bulkClose should arguably enforce canApprove + a legal-state guard consistent with the approve path. Unresolved until the orchestrator/product adjudicates; tests must assert observed behavior labelled characterization, never the intended one.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-BULK-003 (characterization-of-suspect).
+
+## R-BULK-004 — Malformed ids value → unhandled NumberFormatException → 500
+- **ID:** R-BULK-004
+- **Statement:** Any non-numeric `ids` value (including the single empty string `ids=`) throws in the parse loop → 500.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:82-85` (`Long.parseLong(raw[i])`, unguarded; `ids=` yields ["" ]).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `ids=abc`, or `ids=1&ids=abc`, or `ids=`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 500; no partial write (parse precedes the DAO call; the UPDATE runs only after all parse).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Suspected robustness defect, F-002 class. Observed from source; execution pending.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-BULK-004 (characterization).
+
+## R-BULK-005 — bulkClose requires no id parameter and bypasses the 404/unknown-action machinery
+- **ID:** R-BULK-005
+- **Statement:** bulkClose is dispatched before id parsing and before the action switch: no `id` param is needed or read, unknown-action 400 and no-such-task 404 never apply to it, and an all-DONE selection (0 rows updated) still returns 302.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:26-29` (early branch); `:31-36,62-64` unreachable for bulkClose; `dao/TaskDao.java:128-131` (empty guard), `:143` (executeUpdate result ignored).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `action=bulkClose`, `ids=4` (seed DONE task).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/tasks`; 0 rows changed; no error; updated_at of task 4 NOT bumped (WHERE excludes DONE).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; no disagreement known beyond F-001.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-BULK-005.

+# DATA rules — schema, seed, mapping, technical files, observable JSP — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c (frozen ~/app)
+
+Scope: `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql`, `seed.sql`, `dao` row mapping, model/db technical classes, JSPs, web.xml. These rules account for behavior that HTTP tests may not be able to execute; each is still mapped in `rules/app-inventory.json`.
+
+## R-DATA-001 — person table constraints
+- **ID:** R-DATA-001
+- **Statement:** person: id identity PK; username VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE; full_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL; password VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL (plaintext, app-compared); role VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL CHECK IN (ADMIN, MANAGER, MEMBER, VIEWER) — the CHECK enumerates legal values only; it grants no rights (canApprove is Java-only, R-APR-003).
+- **Source citations:** `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql:9-16`; login comparison `dao/PersonDao.java:16-29`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** DB layer; reachable indirectly through login (R-AUTH-002/003) and FK references.
+- **Trigger:** INSERT/UPDATE via SQL (app has no person-mutation route — observed: no HTTP path writes person).
+- **Effects or refusal:** duplicates of username rejected by UNIQUE; illegal role rejected by CHECK.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; username UNIQUE contrasts with project.name (R-PROJ-004).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-DATA-001 (schema echo assertions in other families' oracles).
+
+## R-DATA-002 — project table constraints
+- **ID:** R-DATA-002
+- **Statement:** project: id identity PK; name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL (no UNIQUE — duplicates legal); description VARCHAR(1024) nullable; state VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE' CHECK IN (ACTIVE, ARCHIVED); owner_id BIGINT NOT NULL FK→person.
+- **Source citations:** `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql:18-26`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** DB layer; written by /projects create/archive (R-PROJ-002/005).
+- **Trigger:** project INSERT/UPDATE through those routes or SQL.
+- **Effects or refusal:** illegal state rejected; overlong name → SQLException → 500 (R-PROJ-002); owner FK enforced.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-DATA-002.
+
+## R-DATA-003 — task table constraints and defaults
+- **ID:** R-DATA-003
+- **Statement:** task: id identity PK; title VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL; description VARCHAR(4000) nullable; status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OPEN' CHECK IN (OPEN, IN_PROGRESS, PENDING_APPROVAL, DONE); priority VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MEDIUM' CHECK IN (LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH); assignee_id BIGINT nullable FK→person; project_id BIGINT NOT NULL FK→project; due_date DATE nullable; updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP. The status CHECK enumerates values but NOT transitions — no DB-level lifecycle enforcement (updateStatus/bulkClose accept any legal value from any state; guards are servlet-only, R-APR-004/R-APR-005 note, R-BULK-002).
+- **Source citations:** `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql:28-42`; servlet-only guards `web/TaskActionServlet.java:41-61,100-109`; raw writes `dao/TaskDao.java:115-125,128-147`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** DB layer; every task mutation routes through here.
+- **Trigger:** task INSERT/UPDATE via form/action routes or SQL.
+- **Effects or refusal:** illegal status/priority values → SQLException → 500 (R-FORM-008); FK violations for unknown assignee/project.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; DB defaults OPEN/MEDIUM are shadowed by the servlet always setting both explicitly (R-FORM-003).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-DATA-003.
+
+## R-DATA-004 — Seed data is the fixture contract
+- **ID:** R-DATA-004
+- **Statement:** Seed (loaded by the Postgres container, not the app): persons 1..4 admin(ADMIN)/mgr(MANAGER)/dev(MEMBER)/view(VIEWER), password=username; projects 1..3 Apollo(ACTIVE, owner 2), Borealis(ACTIVE, owner 1), Legacy Archive(ARCHIVED, owner 1); tasks 1..4 = Design login page (OPEN/HIGH/assignee 3/project 1/due 2026-06-15), Wire up SSO (IN_PROGRESS/HIGH/3/1/2026-06-20), Migrate invoices (PENDING_APPROVAL/MEDIUM/3/2/2026-06-10), Archive old data (DONE/LOW/1/2/NULL), with updated_at staggered 09:00/12:00/15:00/18:00 — making the default sort deterministically 4,3,2,1.
+- **Source citations:** `src/main/resources/db/seed.sql:1-21` (stagger comment `:14-16`); loader note `db/Database.java:11-13` and `db/BootstrapListener.java:10-13`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** fresh/reset DB (harness reset re-seeds).
+- **Trigger:** every test's reset.
+- **Effects or refusal:** fixture facts as tabulated in evidence/analyst/fixtures.md (run 01, inherited, unchanged).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; fixtures.md remains accurate (verified against seed.sql — no correction needed).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** all S-* fixtures.
+
+## R-DATA-005 — Row mapping conventions (TaskDao.map)
+- **ID:** R-DATA-005
+- **Statement:** task rows map: assignee_id via getLong + wasNull → Long or null; due_date → Date.toString() ("yyyy-MM-dd") or null; updated_at → Timestamp.toString() truncated to 19 chars ("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", fractional seconds dropped); joined assignee_name/project_name carried for display; person rows map role through Role.valueOf (unknown role string would throw — prevented by the CHECK).
+- **Source citations:** `dao/TaskDao.java:220-238` (assignee `:227-228`, due `:232-233`, updated truncation `:234-236`); `dao/PersonDao.java:46-53` (Role.valueOf `:51`); `dao/ProjectDao.java:86-95`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** every read path (list, form prefill, API).
+- **Trigger:** any query.
+- **Effects or refusal:** observable formats as stated (updatedAt in API and views has no fraction).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-DATA-004.
+
+## R-DATA-006 — Technical model classes (no HTTP surface)
+- **ID:** R-DATA-006
+- **Statement:** Person, Project, Task are plain mutable POJOs (getters/setters only); Role is an enum whose only logic is canApprove() = ADMIN||MANAGER (decides R-APR-001/002; grants nothing else — VIEWER/MEMBER differ from ADMIN/MANAGER only there); TaskStatus is an uninstantiable constants holder (private ctor) — statuses remain stringly-typed and compared with .equals in the servlet layer; none of these classes has behavior reachable by HTTP beyond what the servlets already exercise.
+- **Source citations:** `model/Person.java:1-20`, `model/Project.java:1-28`, `model/Task.java:1-48`, `model/Role.java:7-16`, `model/TaskStatus.java:9-17`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** internal.
+- **Trigger:** n/a.
+- **Effects or refusal:** n/a (accounted in inventory; branches = Role.canApprove's two returns, covered via APR tests).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; stringly-typed statuses noted as technical debt, not a defect.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-DATA-005.
+
+## R-DATA-007 — Startup plumbing: BootstrapListener + Database (unreachable through HTTP)
+- **ID:** R-DATA-007
+- **Statement:** On context init, BootstrapListener calls Database.awaitReady, which retries SELECT 1 up to 30 times at 1s intervals and throws RuntimeException on exhaustion; InterruptedException re-interrupts and returns silently; connection settings come from env DB_URL/DB_USER/DB_PASSWORD with defaults jdbc:postgresql://db:5432/tracker / tracker / tracker; driver registration happens in a static initializer (failure → IllegalStateException). Schema/seed are owned by the DB container — the app never creates tables. None of these branches is reachable by an HTTP request; they execute only at deployment.
+- **Source citations:** `db/BootstrapListener.java:9-18`; `db/Database.java:15-64` (env `:17-19`, static init `:21-28`, getConnection `:33-35`, awaitReady `:42-59`, InterruptedException `:52-55`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** container startup (web.xml listener wiring `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/web.xml:11-13`).
+- **Trigger:** deployment.
+- **Effects or refusal:** app either serves after DB ready or fails startup; no request-time behavior.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; inventory records these as no-test-possible branches (coverage will never reach them via HTTP).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-DATA-005.
+
+## R-DATA-008 — Dead code: TaskDao.findByProject and ProjectDao.findById have no callers
+- **ID:** R-DATA-008
+- **Statement:** TaskDao.findByProject (dao/TaskDao.java:31-33) and ProjectDao.findById (dao/ProjectDao.java:33-43) are not invoked by any servlet or other class — unreachable through HTTP by construction (verified by grep over all 18 sources: only TaskDao.findById is called, from TaskFormServlet.java:30 and TaskActionServlet.java:32).
+- **Source citations:** as above; call-site grep recorded in evidence/analyst/d002-run02-notes.md §DATA-008.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** n/a.
+- **Trigger:** none possible.
+- **Effects or refusal:** n/a.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed dead code (technical debt); accounted in inventory as no-executable-counter source, per family charter.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-DATA-005.
+
+## R-DATA-009 — Observable JSP behavior and directly-reachable static routes
+- **ID:** R-DATA-009
+- **Statement:** Views: tasks.jsp renders per-status transition buttons only for the legal next action of each row's current status (OPEN→Start, IN_PROGRESS→Submit, PENDING_APPROVAL→Approve/Reject, DONE→Reopen — `tasks.jsp:57-69`) using per-row `act-<id>` forms (`:81-86`) and a bulkClose form with `ids` checkboxes (`:38-47`); task-form.jsp shows the Saved banner on `param.saved=='1'` (`:29`), `(unassigned)` for empty assignee (`:37`), `—` for empty due date (`:39`), and the same status-conditional buttons with `return=detail` (`:43-58`); _header.jspf shows `fullName (ROLE)` and the Logout link (`:13-15`); login.jsp shows the error paragraph only when set (`:20`). Directly-reachable static routes beyond the servlet mappings: `/index.jsp` (redirect, R-AUTH-008) and `/styles.css` (stylesheet; auth-exempt via the `.css` suffix, R-AUTH-004); everything under /WEB-INF is unreachable by URL.
+- **Source citations:** `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/tasks.jsp:38-86`, `task-form.jsp:29-59`, `_header.jspf:1-17`, `login.jsp:20`, `index.jsp:1`, `styles.css` (cosmetic only); web.xml servlet mappings `:26-87`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated users (authenticated views).
+- **Trigger:** GET /tasks, /tasks/form[?id], /projects.
+- **Effects or refusal:** rendered HTML as described — the transition-button conditionals make illegal transitions unclickable in the UI, but the servlet guards (R-LIFE-005) remain the only enforcement for crafted requests.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; UI button gating is cosmetic, not authorization (do not infer permission from button absence).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-DATA-006.

+# FORM rules — task form display/create/edit/validation/defaults — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c (frozen ~/app)
+
+Scope: `TaskFormServlet`, `task-form.jsp`, `TaskDao.create/update`. Paths relative to `src/main/java/com/acme/tracker/` unless noted. Status defaults are FORM-family facts; lifecycle transitions are LIFE (rules/R-LIFE.md).
+
+## R-FORM-001 — GET /tasks/form blank display; UI apparent priority default is LOW while server default is MEDIUM
+- **ID:** R-FORM-001
+- **Statement:** GET /tasks/form without id renders a blank "New task" form (200) with project and assignee selects populated; the priority select shows LOW as the visually selected option (first option, no `selected` marker), but a POST that omits the priority field entirely defaults to MEDIUM.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskFormServlet.java:26-35` (no `task` attribute set when no id; `projects`/`people` set `:32-33`); `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/task-form.jsp:27` (New task title), `:96-103` (priority select iterates LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH — first = LOW; `${task.priority == pr}` empty for blank form); server default `web/TaskFormServlet.java:58,89-91` (`orDefault(priority,"MEDIUM")`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user (filter protects /tasks/form).
+- **Trigger:** `GET /tasks/form`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200 blank form. A browser submitting unchanged sends `priority=LOW` (visible selection); a crafted POST without the field gets MEDIUM (`:58`).
+- **Observed versus intended:** **UI/server default disagreement (observed):** displayed default LOW ≠ server-side fallback MEDIUM. Not adjudicated — record; tests should pin both legs separately.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-FORM-001.
+
+## R-FORM-002 — GET /tasks/form?id=N prefill; unknown id silently shows blank form; malformed id → 500
+- **ID:** R-FORM-002
+- **Statement:** With `id` present and non-empty, the servlet loads the task (findById) and prefills the form; an unknown id (null return) leaves the form blank with a "New task" heading and no error; a non-numeric id throws NumberFormatException → 500.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskFormServlet.java:28-31` (`Long.parseLong(idParam)` at `:30`, unguarded); `dao/TaskDao.java:26-29` (findById → null when absent); `task-form.jsp:6,27` (heading falls back to "New task" when task empty).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user.
+- **Trigger:** `GET /tasks/form?id=2` (prefill), `?id=999` (blank, 200), `?id=abc` (500).
+- **Effects or refusal:** Prefill: title/description/project-selected/assignee-selected/priority-selected/dueDate populated (`task-form.jsp:64-108`); detail panel with status badge and status-conditional transitions shown (`:32-62`). Unknown id: 200 blank form (misleading but harmless). Malformed: 500 (F-002 class).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Unknown-id silent blank = observed; 404 might be intended — unresolved, record only. Malformed id = F-002-class suspected defect.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-FORM-002.
+
+## R-FORM-003 — POST create happy path: task starts OPEN, priority defaults MEDIUM, assignee nullable, redirect to saved detail
+- **ID:** R-FORM-003
+- **Statement:** POST /tasks/form without id, valid title + existing projectId, creates a task with status OPEN (forced by servlet, not the form), priority MEDIUM when the field is absent/empty, assignee null when absent/empty/"(unassigned)" value, and redirects 302 to `/tasks/form?id=<newId>&saved=1`.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskFormServlet.java:55-74` (`setStatus(TaskStatus.OPEN)` `:66`; `orDefault(priority,"MEDIUM")` `:58`; assignee null when empty `:60-61`; redirect `:74`); insert `dao/TaskDao.java:75-95` (RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS); DB defaults `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql:32-35` (status DEFAULT OPEN, priority DEFAULT MEDIUM — never used by this path since the servlet always sets both); saved banner `task-form.jsp:29` (`param.saved == '1'`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user (no role gate — VIEWER can create tasks; observed).
+- **Trigger:** `POST /tasks/form` title="X" projectId=1 [assigneeId= [priority= [dueDate=]]].
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `Location: /tasks/form?id=<generated>&saved=1`; new row status OPEN; `updated_at` = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP; "(unassigned)" select option posts empty string → stored NULL (schema allows, `schema.sql:36`).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; no disagreement known.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-FORM-003.
+
+## R-FORM-004 — POST with blank title → 200 re-render "Title is required"; entered input is lost
+- **ID:** R-FORM-004
+- **Statement:** Title is required: absent, empty, or whitespace-only title (after trim) re-renders the form (200) with error "Title is required"; the fail path does not echo back any submitted fields.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskFormServlet.java:41-48` (trim + `title.isEmpty()` → fail); `fail()` `:77-83` (sets error + projects + people; does NOT set task/title/description attributes); `task-form.jsp:28` (error paragraph), `:69,74` (inputs bound to `${task.*}` — empty on fail path).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user.
+- **Trigger:** `POST /tasks/form` with `title=""` or `title="   "` (with description text filled in).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200, "Title is required" shown; description and other entered values NOT preserved (re-render is blank); no DB write.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed = 200 (contrast R-PROJ-003's 400). Input-loss on validation failure is an observed UX defect candidate (recorded; low severity, not adjudicated).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-FORM-004.
+
+## R-FORM-005 — POST with missing/nonexistent projectId → 200 re-render "A task must belong to an existing project"; archived projects accepted; malformed id → 500
+- **ID:** R-FORM-005
+- **Statement:** projectId must be present and belong to an existing project (existence checked via ProjectDao.exists, which ignores state); blank or unknown projectId re-renders with "A task must belong to an existing project"; an ARCHIVED project passes the check; non-numeric projectId throws NFE → 500.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskFormServlet.java:49-53` (`projectIdParam == null || isEmpty || !projects.exists(parseLong(...))` → fail); `dao/ProjectDao.java:45-55` (exists — no state filter); archived seed project `src/main/resources/db/seed.sql:12` (id 3 ARCHIVED).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user.
+- **Trigger:** `projectId` absent / empty / `999` (200 re-render), `projectId=3` (accepted, archived), `projectId=abc` (500 at `:50` parseLong inside the condition).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200 re-render for blank/unknown; 302 saved for archived project (task created in an ARCHIVED project — observed, allowed); 500 for malformed.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Archived-project acceptance is observed behavior; per the no-inference rule, no task-creation restriction is inferred from archival state. Intended policy unresolved — record.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-FORM-005.
+
+## R-FORM-006 — POST edit happy path: fields updated, status preserved, but project_id is silently NOT updated
+- **ID:** R-FORM-006
+- **Statement:** POST /tasks/form with a valid id updates title, description, priority, assignee_id, due_date and bumps updated_at; the UPDATE statement does not include project_id, so a project change made in the form is silently ignored; status is also outside the SET list and thus preserved.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskFormServlet.java:64-72` (edit branch sets id, calls `tasks.update(t)` — projectId set on the object at `:59` but never persisted); `dao/TaskDao.java:97-112` (`UPDATE task SET title=?, description=?, priority=?, assignee_id=?, due_date=?, updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id=?` — no `project_id`, no `status`); form offers Project select on edit `task-form.jsp:79-84`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user; existing task id.
+- **Trigger:** `POST /tasks/form` id=1 projectId=2 title="..." (project changed 1→2).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/tasks/form?id=1&saved=1` ("Saved." banner shown) but oracle `SELECT project_id FROM task WHERE id=1` still 1; detail panel still shows old project. Status unchanged by edit (observable via oracle).
+- **Observed versus intended:** **Defect candidate (observed): form offers a project change that never persists, while the UI confirms "Saved."** Intended presumably to move the task. Repro recipe in evidence/analyst/d002-run02-notes.md §FORM-006; suggested finding F-006 (orchestrator adjudicates). The status-preservation half is benign/intended-looking (status is lifecycle-owned).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-FORM-006 (characterization-of-suspect for the project_id leg).
+
+## R-FORM-007 — POST edit of a nonexistent id → 302 "saved=1" with nothing persisted (silent no-op)
+- **ID:** R-FORM-007
+- **Statement:** The edit branch performs no existence check: updating id 999 runs a 0-row UPDATE and still redirects to `/tasks/form?id=999&saved=1`, showing the Saved banner; a follow-up GET shows a blank form.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskFormServlet.java:68-74` (savedId = parseLong(idParam); update; redirect unconditionally); `dao/TaskDao.java:97-112` (executeUpdate result ignored); `task-form.jsp:29` (Saved banner on param).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user.
+- **Trigger:** `POST /tasks/form` id=999 title="Ghost" projectId=1.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `Location: /tasks/form?id=999&saved=1`; oracle row count for task unchanged (no row 999); banner "✓ Saved." displayed on a task that does not exist.
+- **Observed versus intended:** **Defect candidate (observed): misleading success confirmation for a no-op.** 404 would arguably be intended — unresolved; repro recipe in notes §FORM-007; suggested finding F-007.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-FORM-007 (characterization-of-suspect).
+
+## R-FORM-008 — Crafted inputs that violate DB constraints or formats → unhandled 500
+- **ID:** R-FORM-008
+- **Statement:** The form layer validates only title presence and project existence; other invalid inputs surface as exceptions → 500: nonexistent assigneeId (FK violation), invalid dueDate format (IllegalArgumentException in java.sql.Date.valueOf), non-whitelisted priority (CHECK violation), overlong title (>256) or description (>4000) (VARCHAR limits).
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskFormServlet.java:55-62` (assignee parsed with no existence check `:60-61`; dueDate passed raw `:62`; priority passed raw `:58`); FKs `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql:40-41`; CHECKs `:33,35`; lengths `:30-31`; `java.sql.Date.valueOf` throws IllegalArgumentException (JDK); SQLExceptions are wrapped and rethrown as RuntimeException in `dao/TaskDao.java:92-94,109-111`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user crafting requests (the browser UI cannot produce these).
+- **Trigger:** e.g. `assigneeId=999`, `dueDate=2026-13-45`, `priority=URGENT`, 300-char title.
+- **Effects or refusal:** HTTP 500; no partial DB write (single INSERT/UPDATE). Malformed id on edit (`id=abc`) → NFE 500 (`:69`), F-002 class.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Suspected robustness defects (F-002 class, server-side validation gaps). Observed from source; execution evidence pending. Characterize as-is.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-FORM-008 (characterization).

+# LIFE rules — task lifecycle transitions & guards — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c (frozen ~/app)
+
+Scope: `TaskActionServlet` non-approve/reject actions. Approve/reject stay in R-APR-001..005 (IDs stable, not duplicated). Paths relative to `src/main/java/com/acme/tracker/`. No restriction inferred from role names: observed — NO role gate exists for start/submit/reopen.
+
+## R-LIFE-001 — start: OPEN → IN_PROGRESS, guard expected OPEN, any authenticated role
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-001
+- **Statement:** POST /tasks/action action=start on an OPEN task sets IN_PROGRESS; no role check (VIEWER included); guard refuses any other status with 409.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:40-43` (case start; guard expected `TaskStatus.OPEN` `:41`; `updateStatus(id, IN_PROGRESS)` `:42`); write path `dao/TaskDao.java:115-125`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user; task exists (404 first at `:33-36`).
+- **Trigger:** `POST /tasks/action` `action=start`, `id` of an OPEN task (seed task 1).
+- **Effects or refusal:** success: 302 (R-LIFE-009), status IN_PROGRESS, updated_at bumped. Wrong state: 409 `Cannot start a task in status <current> (expected OPEN)` (guard `:100-109`).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; matches the same guard shape already accepted for approve/reject (R-APR-004).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-LIFE-001.
+
+## R-LIFE-002 — submit: IN_PROGRESS → PENDING_APPROVAL, any authenticated role
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-002
+- **Statement:** action=submit on an IN_PROGRESS task sets PENDING_APPROVAL; no role check; guard refuses other statuses 409.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:44-47` (guard IN_PROGRESS `:45`, `updateStatus(PENDING_APPROVAL)` `:46`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user; task exists.
+- **Trigger:** `action=submit`, id of an IN_PROGRESS task (seed task 2).
+- **Effects or refusal:** success: 302, PENDING_APPROVAL, updated_at bumped; wrong state (e.g. OPEN) → 409 `Cannot submit a task in status OPEN (expected IN_PROGRESS)`.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-LIFE-002.
+
+## R-LIFE-003 — reopen: DONE → OPEN, any authenticated role
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-003
+- **Statement:** action=reopen on a DONE task sets OPEN; no role check; guard refuses other statuses 409.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:58-61` (guard DONE `:59`, `updateStatus(OPEN)` `:60`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user (VIEWER can reopen — observed); task exists.
+- **Trigger:** `action=reopen`, id of a DONE task (seed task 4).
+- **Effects or refusal:** success: 302, OPEN, updated_at bumped; wrong state → 409 `Cannot reopen a task in status <current> (expected DONE)`.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; explicitly: VIEWER is NOT read-only anywhere in this servlet (only approve/reject are gated, by filter/servlet R-APR-001/002).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-LIFE-003.
+
+## R-LIFE-004 — approve/reject remain governed by R-APR-001..005 (cross-reference only)
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-004
+- **Statement:** The approve and reject transitions keep their inherited rules and IDs: role gates R-APR-001/002/003, state guard R-APR-004, effects R-APR-005. Nothing in run 02 supersedes them.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:48-57`; see `rules/R-APR.md` (stable).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** per R-APR.
+- **Trigger:** action=approve / action=reject.
+- **Effects or refusal:** per R-APR (DONE / IN_PROGRESS; 403/409/404).
+- **Observed versus intended:** unchanged; run-01 verifier evidence (ACCEPTED at 340dc91) still applies.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-APR-001..005.
+
+## R-LIFE-005 — General wrong-state guard shape (409, no write) for all single-task actions
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-005
+- **Statement:** Every single-task action checks current status equals its expected value before writing; on mismatch: 409 `Cannot <action> a task in status <current> (expected <expected>)`, no DAO write, updated_at untouched.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:100-109` (guard), invoked `:41,45,50,55,59`; expectations OPEN/IN_PROGRESS/PENDING_APPROVAL×2/DONE.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated; task exists.
+- **Trigger:** any lifecycle action on a task whose status differs from the action's expectation.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 409; oracle row byte-identical (no updated_at bump — no write occurs).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; generalizes the accepted R-APR-004 behavior.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-LIFE-004.
+
+## R-LIFE-006 — Nonexistent task id → 404 "No such task" (after action-specific gates)
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-006
+- **Statement:** An unknown id yields 404 `No such task`; for start/submit/reopen any authenticated role gets 404; for approve/reject a non-approver gets 403 first (filter, R-APR-001/R-APR-002 ordering note).
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:31-36` (parse + findById + 404); filter ordering `web/AuthFilter.java:40-47` vs `:31-36` (documented in R-APR-002 disagreement record).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `action=start`, `id=999` (any role) — 404; `action=approve`, `id=999` as dev — 403.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 404 `No such task` / 403 per ordering; no DB write.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; ordering cross-checks already in evidence/analyst/fixtures.md (run 01).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-LIFE-005.
+
+## R-LIFE-007 — Unknown or missing action → 400 "Unknown action: <action>" (incl. literal "null")
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-007
+- **Statement:** An action value outside {start,submit,approve,reject,reopen} (bulkClose handled separately, R-BULK-001..005) responds 400 `Unknown action: <action>`; a missing action parameter produces the literal message `Unknown action: null` (switch default on "").
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:39` (`switch (action == null ? "" : action)`), `:62-64` (default → 400 `"Unknown action: " + action` — null concatenates as "null").
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated (and, for approve/reject-shaped values only, filter gate; unknown strings pass the filter untouched, `web/AuthFilter.java:42`).
+- **Trigger:** `action=pause`; no action param but `id=1`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 400 with the exact message; no DB write. Note: unknown action + missing id → the 500 at `:31` fires BEFORE the switch (parse precedes dispatch) — only requests carrying a parseable id reach the 400.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; 400 for unknown action is the app's own convention (contrast PROJ/FORM).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-LIFE-006.
+
+## R-LIFE-008 — Missing/malformed id → unhandled NumberFormatException → 500
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-008
+- **Statement:** For non-bulkClose actions, `id` absent or non-numeric throws at `Long.parseLong` → 500 (inherited F-002, unchanged).
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:31`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `action=start` without id, or `id=abc`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 500; no DB write.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Suspected defect (F-002) — observed source behavior, reproduction pending (recipe in findings/ledger.json F-002).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-LIFE-007 (characterization).
+
+## R-LIFE-009 — Success redirect: /tasks by default, /tasks/form?id=<id> when return=detail
+- **ID:** R-LIFE-009
+- **Statement:** After a successful single-task transition the servlet redirects 302 to `<ctx>/tasks`, or to `<ctx>/tasks/form?id=<id>` when `return=detail` (detail-page buttons, task-form.jsp:43-45).
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskActionServlet.java:66-73` (isCommitted check; `"detail".equals(req.getParameter("return"))`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** a transition that succeeded (no earlier sendError committed the response).
+- **Trigger:** any successful action, with/without `return=detail`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 Location as above; status persisted.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed (already covered for approve/reject in R-APR-005; generalized here for start/submit/reopen).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-LIFE-001..003 (redirect assertion included).

+# PROJ rules — projects list/create/archive/validation — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c (frozen ~/app)
+
+Scope: `ProjectServlet`, `ProjectDao`, `projects.jsp`, `project` table. No restriction is inferred from role names or project archival state. Paths relative to `src/main/java/com/acme/tracker/` unless noted.
+
+## R-PROJ-001 — GET /projects lists every project, including ARCHIVED, ordered by name
+- **ID:** R-PROJ-001
+- **Statement:** GET /projects (authenticated) renders all projects ordered by `p.name` with owner full names; archived projects are included and shown.
+- **Source citations:** `web/ProjectServlet.java:23-28`; `dao/ProjectDao.java:19-31` (`BASE ... JOIN person o` `:15-17`, `ORDER BY p.name` `:22`); view `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/projects.jsp:15-36`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user (all four roles — no role check in servlet or filter for /projects).
+- **Trigger:** `GET /projects`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200 HTML table of all projects (seed: Apollo, Borealis, Legacy Archive — alphabetical); Actions column shows Archive button only for `state == 'ACTIVE'` (projects.jsp:25-31) and a Delete link always (projects.jsp:32, see R-PROJ-007).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; no disagreement known.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-PROJ-001.
+
+## R-PROJ-002 — POST /projects create: valid name → new ACTIVE project owned by the session user
+- **ID:** R-PROJ-002
+- **Statement:** POST /projects (no `action` param) with a non-blank `name` creates a project with state ACTIVE, owner = session user, optional description, and redirects 302 to /projects.
+- **Source citations:** `web/ProjectServlet.java:38-50` (name validation `:40-43`; owner from session `:44-48`); `dao/ProjectDao.java:57-72` (INSERT hardcodes `'ACTIVE'` `:58`, RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS); schema `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql:18-26`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user — observed: no role gate (VIEWER can create projects).
+- **Trigger:** `POST /projects` with `name` non-blank after trim (name is stored trimmed, `ProjectServlet.java:46`).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/projects`; new row `state='ACTIVE'`, `owner_id` = session user id; description may be null (column nullable, schema.sql:21). Name > 128 chars → SQLException → unhandled 500 (VARCHAR(128), schema.sql:20).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; intended unknown for role policy (none in source).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-PROJ-002.
+
+## R-PROJ-003 — POST /projects create with blank/missing name → 400 "Project name is required"
+- **ID:** R-PROJ-003
+- **Statement:** Create with `name` absent, empty, or whitespace-only responds 400 with message "Project name is required"; nothing persisted.
+- **Source citations:** `web/ProjectServlet.java:40-43` (`name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()` → `sendError(400, "Project name is required")`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user.
+- **Trigger:** `POST /projects` without action and with blank `name` (also `name="   "`).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 400, no DB write. Note: unlike task-form validation (R-FORM-004, 200 re-render), project validation uses sendError (400).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; consistent with 400 used for unknown actions (R-LIFE-007).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-PROJ-003.
+
+## R-PROJ-004 — Duplicate project names are accepted (no app check, no DB uniqueness)
+- **ID:** R-PROJ-004
+- **Statement:** Creating a project whose name equals an existing project's name succeeds (302, second row persisted); neither servlet nor schema rejects duplicates.
+- **Source citations:** `web/ProjectServlet.java:38-50` (no duplicate check); `src/main/resources/db/schema.sql:20` (`name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL` — no UNIQUE, contrast `person.username UNIQUE` at `:11`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user; an existing project with the same name.
+- **Trigger:** `POST /projects` with `name=Apollo` (existing seed name).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/projects`; two ACTIVE "Apollo" rows; list shows both (R-PROJ-001).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed = duplicates allowed. Whether uniqueness is intended is UNRESOLVED (nothing in source states it); recorded, not adjudicated. Flagged so tests characterize, not prescribe.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-PROJ-004 (characterization).
+
+## R-PROJ-005 — POST /projects action=archive: ACTIVE → ARCHIVED, 302, any authenticated role
+- **ID:** R-PROJ-005
+- **Statement:** POST /projects with `action=archive` and an existing ACTIVE project id sets state to ARCHIVED and redirects 302 to /projects; no role check; no guard other than current-state in the WHERE clause.
+- **Source citations:** `web/ProjectServlet.java:33-37`; `dao/ProjectDao.java:75-84` (`UPDATE project SET state='ARCHIVED' WHERE id=? AND state='ACTIVE'`); view button `projects.jsp:25-31`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** any authenticated user (VIEWER included — observed, no gate).
+- **Trigger:** `POST /projects` `action=archive`, `id` of an ACTIVE project.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/projects`; row state ACTIVE→ARCHIVED. No route restores ACTIVE (no un-archive anywhere in source). Archiving imposes no restriction on the project's tasks (no code reads project.state outside projects.jsp rendering — verified).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; archival is display-only for tasks (do not infer task-mutation restrictions from archival state).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-PROJ-005.

+## R-PROJ-006 — Archive is idempotent-silent: ARCHIVED or nonexistent id still returns 302 with no effect
+- **ID:** R-PROJ-006
+- **Statement:** action=archive on an already-ARCHIVED or nonexistent project id performs a 0-row UPDATE and still responds 302 /projects — no error, no not-found.
+- **Source citations:** `web/ProjectServlet.java:33-37` (no existence check, always redirect); `dao/ProjectDao.java:75-84` (0 rows when `state != 'ACTIVE'` or id absent).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user.
+- **Trigger:** `action=archive` with id of Legacy Archive (already ARCHIVED, seed id 3) or id 999.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 302 `/projects`; oracle `SELECT id,state FROM project ORDER BY id` unchanged.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; candidate intended-behavior question (404/409?) recorded as unresolved-intent note, not asserted.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-PROJ-006.
+
+## R-PROJ-007 — View renders a Delete link to /projects/delete, which has no backing route (404)
+- **ID:** R-PROJ-007
+- **Statement:** projects.jsp renders `<a href="<ctx>/projects/delete?id=N">Delete</a>` for every project, but no servlet is mapped to /projects/delete in web.xml — authenticated requests get 404; anonymous get 302 /login first.
+- **Source citations:** `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/projects.jsp:32`; servlet mappings `src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/web.xml:26-87` (no /projects/delete); filter `web/AuthFilter.java:28-38`.
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user clicking Delete.
+- **Trigger:** `GET /projects/delete?id=1`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 404 (container default servlet); no DB effect. Anonymous: 302 `/login`.
+- **Observed versus intended:** **Source/document disagreement — defect candidate (dead UI control / unimplemented feature).** Repro recipe in evidence/analyst/d002-run02-notes.md §PROJ-007. Suggested finding F-005 (orchestrator adjudicates numbering).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-PROJ-007 (characterization of the 404).
+
+## R-PROJ-008 — Malformed/missing id on archive → unhandled NumberFormatException → 500
+- **ID:** R-PROJ-008
+- **Statement:** action=archive with `id` missing or non-numeric throws NumberFormatException at Long.parseLong → HTTP 500.
+- **Source citations:** `web/ProjectServlet.java:34` (`Long.parseLong(req.getParameter("id"))`, unguarded).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated user.

+- **Effects or refusal:** 500; no DB write. Same defect class as inherited F-002 (G-APR-002) on /tasks/action — extend the finding's scope, don't fork it silently.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Suspected robustness defect (observed source behavior; execution evidence pending). Candidate 400 intended.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-PROJ-008 (characterization).

+# QUERY rules — /tasks filters, sorts, ordering — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c (frozen ~/app)
+
+Scope: `TaskListServlet`, `TaskDao.search/orderClause`, tasks.jsp toolbar. Seed updated_at staggered (09:00/12:00/15:00/18:00, seed.sql:18-21) makes default order deterministic: ids 4,3,2,1. Paths relative to `src/main/java/com/acme/tracker/`.
+
+## R-QUERY-001 — No params → all tasks, default order most-recently-updated first
+- **ID:** R-QUERY-001
+- **Statement:** GET /tasks with no status/assignee/sort returns every task ordered `t.updated_at DESC`.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskListServlet.java:24-37` (null filters → search(null,null,null)); `dao/TaskDao.java:40-56` (dynamic WHERE skipped when no filters); default clause `dao/TaskDao.java:171` (`return "t.updated_at DESC"`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `GET /tasks`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200; seeded order task ids 4,3,2,1; toolbar shows "(all)" status and "Recently updated" selected (sortBy defaulting, TaskListServlet.java:36; tasks.jsp:27).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-QUERY-001.
+
+## R-QUERY-002 — Status filter: exact match, parameterized; empty = no filter; unknown value = empty list (200)
+- **ID:** R-QUERY-002
+- **Statement:** `status` param, when non-empty, filters `t.status = ?` exactly (parameterized — injection-safe); empty/absent means no filter; a value outside the four statuses matches nothing and renders an empty table with 200.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskListServlet.java:24`; `dao/TaskDao.java:44-47` (parameter binding `:46`, `:60-62`); UI select enumerates only the four statuses (`src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/tasks.jsp:18-23`).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `?status=OPEN`; `?status=` (no filter); `?status=BOGUS` (empty list).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200 in all cases; OPEN → task 1 only; BOGUS → no rows; filter select echoes the value (`statusFilter`, tasks.jsp:21).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; invalid status is not validated (no 400) — silent empty result.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-QUERY-002.
+
+## R-QUERY-003 — Assignee filter: person-id param only; empty = no filter; malformed → 500; NULL/unassigned filter unreachable; not exposed in UI
+- **ID:** R-QUERY-003
+- **Statement:** `assignee` param, when non-empty, is parsed with Long.valueOf and filters `t.assignee_id = ?`; empty/absent = no filter; non-numeric throws NFE → 500; there is NO token meaning "unassigned" (assignee_id IS NULL is unreachable through this param); the UI toolbar offers no assignee control (craft-only parameter).
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskListServlet.java:25,31` (`Long.valueOf(assigneeParam)` unguarded); `dao/TaskDao.java:48-51`; toolbar lacks assignee control (`src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/tasks.jsp:15-36` — only status + sort).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated; assignee ids 1..4 (persons).
+- **Trigger:** `?assignee=3` (dev's tasks: 1,2,3); `?assignee=` (all); `?assignee=abc` (500); `?assignee=999` (empty list, no person 999).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200 for numeric filters (possibly empty); 500 for malformed. Unassigned tasks (none in seed; creatable via form) cannot be isolated by any assignee param value.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; the 500 is an F-002-class suspected defect; the missing unassigned filter is an observed capability gap (intent unknown — record).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-QUERY-003.
+
+## R-QUERY-004 — Combined filters AND-join
+- **ID:** R-QUERY-004
+- **Statement:** status and assignee filters combine with SQL AND; both are parameterized; sort applies on top.
+- **Source citations:** `web/TaskListServlet.java:24-32`; `dao/TaskDao.java:52-54` (`String.join(" AND ", where)`), `:56` (ORDER BY appended after WHERE).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `?status=OPEN&assignee=3` (seed → task 1).
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200, tasks matching both; `?status=OPEN&assignee=1` → empty (task 4 is assignee 1 but DONE).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-QUERY-004.
+
+## R-QUERY-005 — Sort whitelist: exact tokens and their SQL clauses
+- **ID:** R-QUERY-005
+- **Statement:** The sort token is never a column name from the request; it maps through a fixed whitelist: `status` → `t.status, t.updated_at DESC`; `priority` → `CASE t.priority WHEN 'HIGH' THEN 0 WHEN 'MEDIUM' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, t.updated_at DESC` (semantic HIGH first); `due` or `due_date` → `t.due_date, t.updated_at DESC`; `title` → `t.title, t.id`; `id` → `t.id`; anything else (incl. null/empty/`updated`) → `t.updated_at DESC`.
+- **Source citations:** `dao/TaskDao.java:154-172` (`orderClause` — whitelist at `:155,158,162,165,168`; default `:171`); concatenation safety comment `:55`; UI options updated/status/priority/due/title/id (`src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/jsp/tasks.jsp:27-32` — note `due_date` is accepted server-side but not offered by the UI).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `?sort=<token>` for each whitelist token.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200 with the corresponding deterministic order on seed data: `status` → 4,2,1,3 (alphabetical DONE<IN_PROGRESS<OPEN<PENDING_APPROVAL); `priority` → 2,1,3,4 (HIGH pair tie-broken by updated DESC); `due` → 3,1,2,4 (dates ascending, NULL due last — Postgres ASC default NULLS LAST); `title` → 4,1,3,2; `id` → 1,2,3,4.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed; the whitelist makes the ORDER BY concatenation injection-safe (only fixed clauses can be appended).
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-QUERY-005.
+
+## R-QUERY-006 — Invalid/unknown sort token falls back to the default order (200, no error)
+- **ID:** R-QUERY-006
+- **Statement:** Any sort value outside the whitelist — including bogus strings, `updated`, empty, and absent — produces the default `t.updated_at DESC` ordering with 200; no validation error. The UI select state (`sortBy`) echoes the raw token unless it is null/empty (then "updated").
+- **Source citations:** `dao/TaskDao.java:171` (default return — comment "default, and explicit \"updated\""); `web/TaskListServlet.java:36` (sortBy null/empty → "updated" for the select).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** `?sort=bogus;DROP` (fallback, harmless), `?sort=updated`, `?sort=`.
+- **Effects or refusal:** 200, order 4,3,2,1 on seed; select shows the raw token as selected when non-default (cosmetic echo).
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed fallback = safe default; no disagreement.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-QUERY-006.
+
+## R-QUERY-007 — Injection safety property (observed, cross-cutting)
+- **ID:** R-QUERY-007
+- **Statement:** status and assignee filter values are bound as PreparedStatement parameters; the only string concatenated into search SQL is the whitelisted ORDER BY clause; no user-controlled column names reach the SQL.
+- **Source citations:** `dao/TaskDao.java:44-51` (params), `:56` (orderClause append), `:60-62` (ps.setObject), `:154-172` (fixed clauses only).
+- **Actors and preconditions:** authenticated.
+- **Trigger:** probes like `?status=OPEN'--`, `?sort=status;--`, `?assignee=3 OR 1=1` (latter → 500 NFE, R-QUERY-003).
+- **Effects or refusal:** filter probes return empty/filtered results unchanged (no SQL effect); sort probes fall back (R-QUERY-006). Recorded as an observed safety property for the QUERY family; no defect.
+- **Observed versus intended:** Observed = safe.
+- **Linked EARS IDs:** S-QUERY-002/003/006 (asserted within those).

+# EARS specs — API — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c
+
+Planned test IDs (T-API-*) are placeholders. Anonymous (no login) for all — the route is auth-exempt (F-003 closed). JSON parsed with a strict parser where noted. Seed tasks 1..4.
+
+## S-API-001 — anonymous full readback
+- **ID:** S-API-001
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /api/tasks` with no session, **the system shall** respond 200 `application/json` with a JSON array of all four tasks in updated_at DESC order (ids 4,3,2,1), each with keys id/title/status/priority/assigneeId/projectId/dueDate/updatedAt and no description key.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-API-001, R-API-006
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** reset DB; anonymous GET.
+- **Observable assertions:** 200; Content-Type application/json (UTF-8); parseable array of 4; key set exactly as listed; id order 4,3,2,1; task 4 has `"assigneeId":null` and `"dueDate":null`.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-API-001 (planned).
+
+## S-API-002 — status filter
+- **ID:** S-API-002
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /api/tasks?status=DONE`, **the system shall** return exactly the DONE tasks (task 4); **and when** status is empty, all tasks; **and when** status is unknown (`BOGUS`), `[]` (200).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-API-002
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** the three probes, anonymous.
+- **Observable assertions:** 200; array sizes 1/4/0; order default.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-API-002 (planned; three legs).
+
+## S-API-003 — escaping and readback (incl. suspect control-character leg)
+- **ID:** S-API-003
+- **EARS statement:** **When** a task title contains `"` or `\`, **the system shall** emit them JSON-escaped (`\"`, `\\`) and HTML-special characters (`<>&`) literally; **and when** a title contains a raw newline, **the system shall** emit it unescaped inside the string (observed; **characterization-of-suspect** — strict-JSON validity defect candidate R-API-003 / suggested F-008).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-API-003
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; create tasks titled `He said "hi"\ & <b>b</b>` and `A%0AB` (form stores titles unmodified); then anonymous GET /api/tasks; reset DB after.
+- **Observable assertions:** escaped leg: title round-trips equal after JSON parse (HTML-specials intact). Newline leg: strict parser REJECTS the body (or body contains a literal 0x0A inside a string) — pin whichever the run observes; label characterization-of-suspect.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-API-003 (planned, characterization for the newline leg).
+
+## S-API-004 — null-field rendering
+- **ID:** S-API-004
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the read-back includes an unassigned task with no due date, **the system shall** render `"assigneeId":null` and `"dueDate":null` as bare tokens, while `updatedAt` is always a quoted non-empty timestamp.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-API-004
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** create a task with no assignee/due (S-FORM-003 fixture), then GET /api/tasks.
+- **Observable assertions:** bare `null` tokens (not `"null"`); updatedAt matches `\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}` (fraction truncated, R-DATA-005).
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-API-004 (planned).
+
+## S-API-005 — GET-only (unwanted behavior)
+- **ID:** S-API-005
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /api/tasks` (anonymous), **then the system shall** respond 405 and access no data.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-API-005
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** anonymous POST; oracle before/after.
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 405; oracle unchanged (supports F-003's no-mutation adjudication).
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-API-005 (planned).

+# EARS specs — AUTH — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c
+
+Planned test IDs (T-AUTH-*) are placeholders — the engineer/verifier supply real executed IDs via the orchestrator; none of the results below are executed evidence. Fixtures: seeded users admin/mgr/dev/view (password = username), seed.sql:2-6.
+
+## S-AUTH-001 — login form display
+- **ID:** S-AUTH-001
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /login`, **the system shall** respond 200 with the sign-in form and no error message.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-AUTH-001
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** none required; fresh anonymous session.
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 200; body contains `Sign in` and a form posting to `/login`; body does NOT contain `Invalid credentials`; no JSESSIONID needed/issued.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-AUTH-001 (planned).
+
+## S-AUTH-002 — login success
+- **ID:** S-AUTH-002
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /login` with `username` and `password` matching a seeded person, **the system shall** create a session carrying that person as `user` and respond 302 to `/tasks`.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-AUTH-002
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** `mgr`/`mgr` (MANAGER) — repeat for `admin`/`admin`, `dev`/`dev`, `view`/`view`.
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 302 `Location: /tasks`; Set-Cookie JSESSIONID; subsequent `GET /tasks` with the cookie returns 200 (not a redirect to /login); header shows `Morgan Manager (MANAGER)` (_header.jspf:13).
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-AUTH-002 (planned; parametrize per user).
+
+## S-AUTH-003 — login failure (unwanted behavior)
+- **ID:** S-AUTH-003
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /login` with credentials matching no person (wrong password, unknown user, or missing/null fields), **then the system shall** respond 200 re-rendering the login page with `Invalid credentials` and issue no working session.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-AUTH-003
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** `mgr`/`wrong`; `nobody`/`x`; empty username; both fields absent.
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 200 (not 401); body contains `Invalid credentials`; a following `GET /tasks` (same cookie jar, if any) redirects 302 `/login`.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-AUTH-003 (planned).
+
+## S-AUTH-004 — anonymous access to protected routes (unwanted behavior)
+- **ID:** S-AUTH-004
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives a GET or POST to any non-exempt path (`/tasks`, `/tasks/form`, `/tasks/action`, `/projects`, `/logout`, `/index.jsp`) without an authenticated session, **then the system shall** respond 302 to `/login` and not invoke any servlet logic.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-AUTH-004, R-AUTH-005
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** anonymous jar; probe each path incl. `POST /tasks/action action=approve id=3` (inherits S-APR-005 evidence from run 01).
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 302 `Location: /login` for every probe; oracle `SELECT id,status FROM task ORDER BY id` unchanged after the POST probes.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-AUTH-004 (planned).
+
+## S-AUTH-005 — exempt paths reachable without authentication
+- **ID:** S-AUTH-005
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives a request to `/login`, any path starting `/api/`, or any path ending `.css`, **the system shall** apply no authentication gate.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-AUTH-004 (cross-family: R-API-001)
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** anonymous `GET /api/tasks`, `GET /styles.css`, `GET /login`.
+- **Observable assertions:** none of the three returns 302 `/login` (API returns JSON, css returns 200 stylesheet, login returns the form).
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-AUTH-005 (planned).
+
+## S-AUTH-006 — logout invalidates the session
+- **ID:** S-AUTH-006
+- **EARS statement:** **While** a session is authenticated, **when** the system receives `GET /logout`, **the system shall** invalidate that session and respond 302 to `/login`; **and when** it receives `POST /logout`, **the system shall** respond 405 and leave the session valid.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-AUTH-006, R-AUTH-007
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `dev`/`dev`; then `GET /logout` with the cookie; replay the same cookie on `/tasks`. Separately: fresh login then `POST /logout`, then `GET /tasks` with same cookie.
+- **Observable assertions:** GET leg: 302 `/login`; replayed cookie on `/tasks` → 302 `/login` (session dead). POST leg: 405; `/tasks` with same cookie → 200 (session still alive).
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-AUTH-006 (planned; two legs).
+
+## S-AUTH-007 — root redirect (characterization with one unresolved hop)
+- **ID:** S-AUTH-007
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /` without a session, **the system shall** redirect such that the client ends at `/login` (first hop `/login` or `/tasks` — container welcome-file dispatch, unresolved, see R-AUTH-008); **and when** it receives `GET /index.jsp` without a session, **the system shall** respond 302 `/login` directly.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-AUTH-008
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** anonymous `GET /`; anonymous `GET /index.jsp`; authenticated `GET /index.jsp`.
+- **Observable assertions:** `GET /index.jsp` anonymous → 302 `Location: /login`; authenticated → 302 `Location: /tasks` (index.jsp:1). `GET /` anonymous → 302 whose Location is one of {`/login`, `/tasks`} — record which, and that following redirects ends at the login form. **This spec deliberately leaves the first-hop Location unasserted pending the probe; the engineer's executed result resolves R-AUTH-008's open question.**
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-AUTH-007 (planned; first-hop recording).

+# EARS specs — BULK — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c
+
+Planned test IDs (T-BULK-*) are placeholders. Oracle: `SELECT id,status,updated_at FROM task ORDER BY id`. Reset DB before mutating cases. **F-001 policy:** every spec below characterizes OBSERVED behavior, explicitly labelled characterization-of-suspect; the INTENDED requirement (canApprove + PENDING_APPROVAL guard consistent with the approve path) is recorded here as UNRESOLVED and is deliberately NOT asserted.
+
+## S-BULK-001 — empty selection no-op
+- **ID:** S-BULK-001
+- **EARS statement:** **When** an authenticated user sends `POST /tasks/action` with `action=bulkClose` and no `ids` values, **the system shall** respond 302 to `/tasks` and change nothing.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-BULK-001
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; POST without ids field (the unchecked-boxes case).
+- **Observable assertions:** 302 `/tasks`; oracle unchanged.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-BULK-001 (planned).
+
+## S-BULK-002 — mixed-status selection (characterization-of-suspect; F-001 execution)
+- **ID:** S-BULK-002
+- **EARS statement:** **When** an authenticated user sends `action=bulkClose` with `ids=1&ids=3&ids=4&ids=999`, **the system shall** respond 302 to `/tasks` with tasks 1 (OPEN) and 3 (PENDING_APPROVAL) set to DONE with bumped `updated_at`, task 4 (already DONE) untouched, and no error for 999 (observed; **characterization-of-suspect** — PENDING_APPROVAL reaching DONE without approval is F-001's suspected defect; **INTENDED, unresolved, not asserted**: only approvers, and only from legal states, should reach DONE).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-BULK-002, R-BULK-005
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; reset DB first. — This executes F-001's pending reproduction for the state dimension.
+- **Observable assertions:** 302; oracle: 1 DONE, 3 DONE, 4 DONE with original updated_at, 2 IN_PROGRESS unchanged.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-BULK-002 (planned, characterization-of-suspect; F-001 repro).
+
+## S-BULK-003 — non-approver role (characterization-of-suspect; F-001 execution)
+- **ID:** S-BULK-003
+- **EARS statement:** **When** a MEMBER or VIEWER sends `action=bulkClose` with `ids=3` (PENDING_APPROVAL), **the system shall** respond 302 and set task 3 to DONE (observed; **characterization-of-suspect** — F-001's role dimension: no canApprove gate on this path; **INTENDED, unresolved, not asserted**: MEMBER/VIEWER should be refused like approve/reject).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-BULK-003 (cross: R-APR-001, R-APR-004)
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `dev`; reset DB; `ids=3`; repeat as `view`.
+- **Observable assertions:** 302 (no 403); oracle task 3 DONE; contrast control: `action=approve&id=3` as dev → 403 (S-APR-003).
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-BULK-003 (planned, characterization-of-suspect; F-001 repro).
+
+## S-BULK-004 — malformed ids value → 500 (characterization-of-suspect)
+- **ID:** S-BULK-004
+- **EARS statement:** **When** an authenticated user sends `action=bulkClose` with any non-numeric `ids` value (including the single empty string), **then the system shall** respond 500 and write nothing (observed; **characterization-of-suspect**, F-002 class).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-BULK-004
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; `ids=abc`; `ids=1&ids=abc`; `ids=`.
+- **Observable assertions:** 500 all three; oracle unchanged (parse precedes the UPDATE).
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-BULK-004 (planned, characterization-of-suspect).
+
+## S-BULK-005 — all-DONE selection → 302, zero changes
+- **ID:** S-BULK-005
+- **EARS statement:** **When** an authenticated user sends `action=bulkClose` with `ids=4` (only a DONE task), **the system shall** respond 302 with no status or `updated_at` change to task 4.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-BULK-005
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; `ids=4`.
+- **Observable assertions:** 302; oracle task 4 status DONE and updated_at byte-identical.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-BULK-005 (planned).

+# EARS specs — DATA — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c
+
+Planned test IDs (T-DATA-*) are placeholders. Most DATA rules have no executable HTTP surface; specs below cover the observable legs, and `rules/app-inventory.json` accounts for the no-test-possible remainder.
+
+## S-DATA-001/002/003 — constraint echo via crafted writes (characterization)
+- **ID:** S-DATA-001 (person), S-DATA-002 (project), S-DATA-003 (task)
+- **EARS statement:** **When** a crafted request would violate a DB constraint (duplicate project name → accepted per S-PROJ-004; unknown assignee / bad priority / overlong title → 500 per S-FORM-008; illegal state value in SQL → rejected by CHECK), **the system shall** behave exactly as the linked family specs characterize, and direct SQL probes against the oracle shall confirm the schema (username UNIQUE, state/status/priority CHECKs, FKs, defaults OPEN/MEDIUM).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-DATA-001, R-DATA-002, R-DATA-003 (cross: R-PROJ-004, R-FORM-008)
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** reset DB; SQL probe script over the oracle connection (harness `runtime.py sql`): attempt `INSERT INTO project(name,owner_id) VALUES('dup',1)` twice; `UPDATE task SET status='BOGUS' WHERE id=1`; `UPDATE person SET role='EMPEROR' WHERE id=1` — all via SELECT-verified error capture where the harness permits.
+- **Observable assertions:** second duplicate-name insert succeeds (no UNIQUE); BOGUS status and EMPEROR role rejected by CHECK (error messages recorded verbatim); defaults visible via `SELECT status,priority FROM task WHERE id=<new-inserted-row>` in a fresh SQL insert probe.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-DATA-001 (planned, oracle-level).
+
+## S-DATA-004 — mapping formats
+- **ID:** S-DATA-004
+- **EARS statement:** **When** tasks are read back through any view or the API, **the system shall** render due_date as `yyyy-MM-dd`, updatedAt as `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss` (fractional seconds truncated), and an absent assignee as `(unassigned)` in the detail view.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-DATA-005 (cross: R-API-004)
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** seeded task 4 (NULL due) and any task's updatedAt via /api/tasks and /tasks/form?id=2.
+- **Observable assertions:** formats as stated in both surfaces.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-DATA-002 (planned).
+
+## S-DATA-005 — technical files accounted, no HTTP execution
+- **ID:** S-DATA-005
+- **EARS statement:** N/A — accounting entry: `Role.canApprove()`'s two returns are executed via APR tests; `TaskStatus` constants via LIFE; POJO getters/setters via every read path; `Database`/`BootstrapListener` startup branches (awaitReady retry/timeout, InterruptedException, driver-missing IllegalStateException) and the dead methods `TaskDao.findByProject`/`ProjectDao.findById` are **unreachable through HTTP** and are recorded as permanent inventory gaps (no test can execute them against the running app).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-DATA-006, R-DATA-007, R-DATA-008
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** none.
+- **Observable assertions:** none (inventory-only; cited in rules/app-inventory.json `gaps`).
+- **Executable test IDs:** none possible — gap GAP-DATA-001/002/003.
+
+## S-DATA-006 — observable JSP behavior
+- **ID:** S-DATA-006
+- **EARS statement:** **When** an authenticated user views /tasks and /tasks/form?id=N, **the system shall** render transition buttons only for each row's current-status-legal action (Start/Submit/Approve+Reject/Reopen), the bulkClose form with `ids` checkboxes, the Saved banner on `saved=1`, and the header `fullName (ROLE)` + Logout link.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-DATA-009
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** seeded DB; login `dev`; GET /tasks; GET /tasks/form?id=2; then a saved redirect (POST edit) for the banner.
+- **Observable assertions:** per-row button sets exactly as the status conditionals (tasks.jsp:57-69; task-form.jsp:46-58); banner `Saved.` appears only with saved=1; header shows `Devon Member (MEMBER)`.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-DATA-003 (planned).

+# EARS specs — FORM — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c
+
+Planned test IDs (T-FORM-*) are placeholders. Oracle: `SELECT id,title,status,priority,assignee_id,project_id,due_date FROM task ORDER BY id` (extend with description when needed). Seed tasks 1..4 (seed.sql:17-21). Login `mgr` unless stated.
+
+## S-FORM-001 — blank form display and defaults (characterization of the LOW/MEDIUM split)
+- **ID:** S-FORM-001
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /tasks/form` without `id`, **the system shall** respond 200 with a blank form titled "New task", populated project and assignee selects, and LOW as the visually preselected priority option.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-FORM-001
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `dev`; GET /tasks/form.

+
+## S-FORM-002 — edit prefill and unknown/malformed id
+- **ID:** S-FORM-002
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /tasks/form?id=2`, **the system shall** respond 200 with task 2's values prefilled and a detail panel; **and when** the id is unknown (999), **the system shall** respond 200 with a blank "New task" form; **and when** the id is non-numeric, **the system shall** respond 500 (characterization-of-suspect, F-002 class).
