+- **Observable assertions:** id=2: title `Wire up SSO`, status badge IN_PROGRESS, project Apollo selected, assignee Devon Member selected, priority HIGH selected, due 2026-06-20; transitions show only Submit-for-approval (task-form.jsp:49-51). id=999: 200, `New task`, no detail panel. id=abc: 500.

+
+## S-FORM-003 — create success with defaults and null assignee
+- **ID:** S-FORM-003
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /tasks/form` (no id) with a valid title and existing projectId, **the system shall** create the task with status OPEN and respond 302 to `/tasks/form?id=<new>&saved=1`; **and when** `priority` is absent, **the system shall** store MEDIUM; **and when** `assigneeId` is empty, **the system shall** store NULL.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-FORM-003, R-FORM-001 (server-default leg)
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `view` (pins no role gate on create); `title=Probe task`, `projectId=1`, no priority, no assignee, no dueDate; follow redirect.
+- **Observable assertions:** 302 Location `/tasks/form?id=5&saved=1` (next identity id); GET of it shows `Saved.` banner + detail panel status OPEN + `(unassigned)`; oracle row 5: status OPEN, priority MEDIUM, assignee_id NULL, project_id 1, due_date NULL. Reset DB after.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-FORM-003 (planned).
+
+## S-FORM-004 — blank title refusal (unwanted behavior; input loss characterized)
+- **ID:** S-FORM-004
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /tasks/form` with `title` absent, empty, or whitespace-only, **then the system shall** respond 200 re-rendering the form with `Title is required`, persist nothing, and not echo the submitted description (observed input-loss, characterization).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-FORM-004
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; `title=`, `description=should vanish`, `projectId=1`; repeat `title=%20%20`.
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 200 (not 400); body contains `Title is required`; textarea empty; oracle row count unchanged.

+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /tasks/form` with `projectId` absent, empty, or unknown (999), **then the system shall** respond 200 with `A task must belong to an existing project`; **and when** `projectId=3` (ARCHIVED), **the system shall** accept and create the task there (observed; no archival restriction); **and when** `projectId=abc`, **the system shall** respond 500 (characterization-of-suspect).

+- **Observable assertions:** 302 + `Saved.` banner; oracle task 1: title updated, project_id still 1 (NOT 2), status still OPEN, priority LOW, assignee_id NULL, updated_at bumped; detail panel still shows project Apollo.

+# EARS specs — LIFE — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c
+
+Planned test IDs (T-LIFE-*) are placeholders. Oracle: `SELECT id,status FROM task ORDER BY id`. Reset DB before each mutating case. Login `mgr` unless stated; `view` used where pinning "no role gate" matters.

+- **EARS statement:** **While** a task is IN_PROGRESS, **when** any authenticated user sends `action=submit` for it, **the system shall** set it to PENDING_APPROVAL, bump `updated_at`, and respond 302.

+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-LIFE-005
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; start on task 2 (IN_PROGRESS) → 409 `Cannot start a task in status IN_PROGRESS (expected OPEN)`; submit on task 1 (OPEN); reopen on task 1 (OPEN).

+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-LIFE-006 (cross: R-APR-001, R-APR-002)

+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /projects` with `action=archive` and the id of an ACTIVE project from any authenticated user, **the system shall** set that project's state to ARCHIVED and respond 302 to `/projects`.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-PROJ-005
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `view`/VIEWER (pins no role gate); `action=archive`, `id=1` (Apollo).
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 302 `/projects`; oracle project 1 state ACTIVE→ARCHIVED; its tasks untouched (`SELECT id,status FROM task` unchanged — archival does not cascade). Reset DB after.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-PROJ-005 (planned).
+
+## S-PROJ-006 — archive idempotence / unknown id (characterization)
+- **ID:** S-PROJ-006
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /projects` with `action=archive` and the id of a project that is ARCHIVED or does not exist, **the system shall** respond 302 to `/projects` with no state change and no error (observed; characterization — intended 404/409 unresolved).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-PROJ-006
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; `action=archive&id=3` (already ARCHIVED); `action=archive&id=999`.
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 302 `/projects` both times; oracle unchanged both times.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-PROJ-006 (planned, characterization).
+
+## S-PROJ-007 — Delete link 404 (characterization-of-suspect)
+- **ID:** S-PROJ-007
+- **EARS statement:** **When** an authenticated user follows the Delete link rendered by `/projects` (`GET /projects/delete?id=N`), **the system shall** respond 404, because no route is mapped (observed; **characterization-of-suspect** — dead UI control, defect candidate R-PROJ-007).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-PROJ-007
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; `GET /projects/delete?id=1`; also anonymous variant.
+- **Observable assertions:** authenticated → 404; anonymous → 302 `/login` (filter first). Oracle unchanged.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-PROJ-007 (planned, characterization-of-suspect).
+
+## S-PROJ-008 — malformed archive id → 500 (characterization-of-suspect)
+- **ID:** S-PROJ-008
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `POST /projects` with `action=archive` and a missing or non-numeric `id`, **the system shall** respond 500 (unhandled NumberFormatException; observed; **characterization-of-suspect**, F-002 class — intended 400 unresolved).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-PROJ-008
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** login `mgr`; `action=archive` with no `id`; `action=archive&id=abc`.
+- **Observable assertions:** HTTP 500 both times; oracle unchanged.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-PROJ-008 (planned, characterization-of-suspect).

+# EARS specs — QUERY — task-tracker-demo @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c
+
+Planned test IDs (T-QUERY-*) are placeholders. Seed default order is ids 4,3,2,1 (updated_at 18:00/15:00/12:00/09:00, seed.sql:18-21). Order assertions read the ID column sequence of GET /tasks HTML (first data row = first). Login `mgr`.
+
+## S-QUERY-001 — default listing and ordering
+- **ID:** S-QUERY-001
+- **EARS statement:** **When** an authenticated user requests `GET /tasks` with no parameters, **the system shall** respond 200 listing all tasks in `updated_at DESC` order (seed: 4,3,2,1) with the toolbar showing "(all)" and "Recently updated".
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-QUERY-001
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** reset DB; GET /tasks.
+- **Observable assertions:** 200; task id sequence 4,3,2,1; status select shows "(all)" selected; sort select "updated" selected.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-QUERY-001 (planned).
+
+## S-QUERY-002 — status filter legs
+- **ID:** S-QUERY-002
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /tasks?status=<S>`, **the system shall** list only tasks with status exactly S (200) — with S=`OPEN` → task 1, S=`BOGUS` → empty table (200, no error), S empty → all tasks; filter values are parameterized (injection probes have no SQL effect).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-QUERY-002, R-QUERY-007
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** `?status=OPEN`, `?status=BOGUS`, `?status=`, `?status=OPEN'--`.
+- **Observable assertions:** 200 each; row sets as stated; select echoes the raw value.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-QUERY-002 (planned; four legs).
+
+## S-QUERY-003 — assignee filter legs (incl. 500 characterization)
+- **ID:** S-QUERY-003
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /tasks?assignee=<id>`, **the system shall** list only that person's tasks (id 3 → tasks 1,2,3; id 999 → empty); **and when** assignee is empty, **the system shall** list all; **and when** assignee is non-numeric, **the system shall** respond 500 (observed; **characterization-of-suspect**, F-002 class). There is no value meaning "unassigned" (capability gap, observed).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-QUERY-003
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** `?assignee=3`, `?assignee=999`, `?assignee=`, `?assignee=abc`.
+- **Observable assertions:** as stated per leg; 200/200/200/500.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-QUERY-003 (planned; four legs).
+
+## S-QUERY-004 — combined filters
+- **ID:** S-QUERY-004
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /tasks?status=OPEN&assignee=3`, **the system shall** list tasks matching BOTH (task 1); **and when** the combination matches nothing (`status=OPEN&assignee=1`), **the system shall** render an empty table (200).
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-QUERY-004
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** the two probes on seeded data.
+- **Observable assertions:** 200; row sets as stated.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-QUERY-004 (planned).
+
+## S-QUERY-005 — every whitelisted sort token
+- **ID:** S-QUERY-005
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /tasks?sort=<token>`, **the system shall** order rows exactly per the whitelist: `status` → 4,2,1,3; `priority` → 2,1,3,4; `due` → 3,1,2,4 (NULL due last); `due_date` (server-only token) → same as `due`; `title` → 4,1,3,2; `id` → 1,2,3,4.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-QUERY-005
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** reset DB; one GET per token (six + `due_date` = seven).
+- **Observable assertions:** 200; exact id sequences per token (sources: TaskDao.java:154-172 clauses; Postgres ASC NULLS LAST for due).
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-QUERY-005 (planned; parametrized seven cases).
+
+## S-QUERY-006 — invalid/unknown sort fallback
+- **ID:** S-QUERY-006
+- **EARS statement:** **When** the system receives `GET /tasks?sort=<value>` with any non-whitelisted value (bogus, injection-shaped, `updated`, empty, or absent), **the system shall** respond 200 with the default `updated_at DESC` order (4,3,2,1) and no error.
+- **Linked rule IDs:** R-QUERY-006, R-QUERY-007
+- **Fixtures and inputs:** `?sort=bogus`, `?sort=status;--`, `?sort=updated`, `?sort=`, no param.
+- **Observable assertions:** 200; order 4,3,2,1 in every leg.
+- **Executable test IDs:** T-QUERY-006 (planned; five legs).

+"""Public JSON API family (run 02, item T-API): GET /api/tasks readback,
+status filter, JSON structure, escaping, null rendering, no-login access,
+non-GET behavior.
+
+Source basis (frozen ~/app @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c):
+- web/ApiTaskServlet.java:23-49 (GET-only; status filter only; hand-rolled
+  JSON; esc() at :51-54 escapes backslash and double-quote ONLY — JSON
+  escaping, never HTML escaping; null assigneeId/dueDate render as JSON
+  null; description is not in the payload)
+- web/AuthFilter.java:28 (/api/* exempt from authentication — F-003,
+  closed not-a-defect)
+- dao/TaskDao.java:22-24 (findAll ORDER BY updated_at DESC)
+Seed (seed.sql:17-21): 4 tasks; API order [4,3,2,1].
+Placeholder EARS IDs (S-API-*/R-API-*) pending analyst batches.
+"""
+import json
+import subprocess
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from httpcase import HttpCase
+
+OUTPUTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+
+EXPECTED_KEYS = {"id", "title", "status", "priority", "assigneeId",
+                 "projectId", "dueDate", "updatedAt"}
+
+
+def reset_db():
+    subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "reset"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    )
+
+
+class TestApiReadback(HttpCase):
+    """S-API-001 (rules R-API-001, R-API-002): anonymous GET /api/tasks ->
+    200 application/json; the 4 seeded tasks as a JSON array ordered
+    updated-DESC [4,3,2,1]; exact key set; typed values (ints, JSON null
+    for task 4's dueDate, trimmed updatedAt string)."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_api_001_readback_structure_and_order(self):
+        self.assertIsNone(self.session_cookie())
+        r = self.request("GET", "/api/tasks")
+        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
+        self.assertTrue(r.headers.get("Content-Type", "").startswith(
+            "application/json"))
+        rows = json.loads(r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], [t["id"] for t in rows])
+        for t in rows:
+            self.assertEqual(EXPECTED_KEYS, set(t))
+        self.assertEqual(
+            {"id": 4, "title": "Archive old data", "status": "DONE",
+             "priority": "LOW", "assigneeId": 1, "projectId": 2,
+             "dueDate": None, "updatedAt": "2026-07-15 18:00:00"},
+            rows[0])
+        self.assertEqual("Migrate invoices", rows[1]["title"])
+        self.assertEqual("PENDING_APPROVAL", rows[1]["status"])
+        self.assertEqual("2026-06-10", rows[1]["dueDate"])
+
+
+class TestApiStatusFilter(HttpCase):
+    """S-API-002 (rule R-API-002): ?status=<s> narrows the readback;
+    unknown status -> empty array."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_api_002_status_filter(self):
+        for status, ids in (("DONE", [4]), ("OPEN", [1]),
+                            ("PENDING_APPROVAL", [3]), ("BOGUS", [])):
+            with self.subTest(status=status):
+                rows = json.loads(self.request(
+                    "GET", "/api/tasks?status=" + status).read().decode())
+                self.assertEqual(ids, [t["id"] for t in rows])
+
+
+class TestApiAssigneeParamIgnored(HttpCase):
+    """S-API-002 (rule R-API-002) — CHARACTERIZATION (observed; no intent
+    claim): ApiTaskServlet reads ONLY the status parameter
+    (ApiTaskServlet.java:24-27); an assignee parameter is silently ignored
+    and the full list comes back, unlike /tasks (TaskListServlet)."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_api_002_assignee_param_ignored(self):
+        rows = json.loads(self.request(
+            "GET", "/api/tasks?assignee=1").read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], [t["id"] for t in rows])
+
+
+class TestApiEscapingAndNulls(HttpCase):
+    """S-API-003/S-API-004 (rule R-API-004): special characters round-trip as valid
+    JSON — esc() JSON-escapes " and \\ so json.loads succeeds and values
+    come back byte-exact; HTML markup is NOT escaped (correct for
+    application/json, observed characterization). Null/empty fields: a task
+    created with empty assignee/dueDate renders assigneeId and dueDate as
+    JSON null; null description simply absent from the payload."""
+
+    TITLE = 'A "quoted" <b>bold</b> \\path'
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_api_003_004_escaping_and_null_rendering(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                         {"title": self.TITLE, "projectId": "1",
+                          "assigneeId": "", "dueDate": ""})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        # Read back anonymously: the API is public (F-003). Rebuilding the
+        # base fixture gives a fresh cookie jar/opener without touching the DB.
+        HttpCase.setUp(self)
+        self.assertIsNone(self.session_cookie())
+        r = self.request("GET", "/api/tasks")
+        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
+        raw = r.read().decode()
+        rows = json.loads(raw)  # must be valid JSON despite the quotes
+        created = next(t for t in rows if t["id"] == 5)
+        self.assertEqual(self.TITLE, created["title"])  # byte-exact
+        self.assertIn("<b>bold</b>", raw)               # no HTML escaping
+        self.assertIsNone(created["assigneeId"])
+        self.assertIsNone(created["dueDate"])
+        self.assertNotIn("description", created)
+
+
+class TestApiNonGetMethods(HttpCase):
+    """S-API-005 (rule R-API-005): ApiTaskServlet overrides only doGet;
+    other methods hit HttpServlet's default -> 405 (observed for POST and
+    PUT), reachable without login (filter exemption)."""
+
+    def test_s_api_005_post_and_put_refused_405(self):
+        self.assertIsNone(self.session_cookie())
+        for method in ("POST", "PUT"):
+            r = self.request(method, "/api/tasks",
+                             {"title": "x"} if method == "POST" else None)
+            self.assertEqual(405, r.status, method)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+"""Auth/session family (run 02, item T-AUTH): logout, anonymous access to

+import subprocess
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+

+def reset_db():
+    subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "reset"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    )
+

+def oracle_statuses():
+    """Read-only oracle: {id: status} from `SELECT id,status FROM task`."""
+    out = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "sql",
+         "SELECT id,status FROM task ORDER BY id"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    ).stdout
+    return {int(line.split("|")[0]): line.split("|")[1].strip()
+            for line in out.strip().splitlines()}
+

+class TestLogoutInvalidatesSession(HttpCase):
+    """S-AUTH-006 (rules R-AUTH-004, R-AUTH-007): after GET /logout the session
+    is invalidated: 302 to /login, and a later GET /tasks on the same cookie
+    jar is refused with 302 to /login (LogoutServlet.java:13-17,
+    AuthFilter.java:33-38)."""
+
+    def test_s_auth_006_logout_then_tasks_redirects_to_login(self):
+        r = self.login("mgr", "mgr")
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual(200, self.request("GET", "/tasks").status)
+        r = self.request("GET", "/logout")
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/login", r.headers.get("Location"))
+        r = self.request("GET", "/tasks")
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/login", r.headers.get("Location"))
+
+
+class TestAnonymousGetProtectedRoutes(HttpCase):
+    """S-AUTH-004 (rule R-AUTH-004): anonymous GET on protected routes

+    (AuthFilter.java:33-38; none of these paths is exempt per
+    AuthFilter.java:28-31)."""
+

+        self.assertIsNone(self.session_cookie())
+        for path in ("/tasks", "/projects", "/tasks/form", "/logout"):
+            r = self.request("GET", path)
+            self.assertEqual(302, r.status, path)
+            self.assertEqual("/login", r.headers.get("Location"), path)

+class TestAnonymousPostNoMutation(HttpCase):
+    """S-AUTH-004 (rule R-AUTH-004): anonymous POST to protected routes
+    (/tasks/action, /tasks/form, /projects) -> 302 Location /login and no
+    persisted mutation (filter runs before any servlet/DAO code)."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()

+        for path in ("/", "/index.jsp"):

+"""Filter/sort family (run 02, item T-QUERY): status filter, assignee
+filter, combined filters, EVERY whitelisted sort token, default ordering,
+invalid-token fallback — asserting the ACTUAL returned row sequence parsed
+from the /tasks HTML, not just 200.
+
+Source basis (frozen ~/app @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c):
+- web/TaskListServlet.java:22-37 (params status/assignee/sort; empty
+  assignee -> null, i.e. NO unassigned-only filter exists)
+- dao/TaskDao.java:40-73 (dynamic WHERE) and :154-172 (orderClause
+  whitelist: status/priority/due/due_date/title/id, default updated DESC)
+Seed (seed.sql:17-21, staggered updated_at): id 1 OPEN HIGH due 06-15
+upd 09:00; id 2 IN_PROGRESS HIGH due 06-20 upd 12:00; id 3
+PENDING_APPROVAL MEDIUM due 06-10 upd 15:00; id 4 DONE LOW due NULL
+upd 18:00. Default order (updated DESC) is therefore [4,3,2,1].
+Placeholder EARS IDs (S-QRY-*/R-QRY-*) pending analyst batches.
+"""
+import re
+import subprocess
+import unittest
+import urllib.parse
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from httpcase import HttpCase
+
+OUTPUTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+
+
+def reset_db():
+    subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "reset"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    )
+
+
+def listed_ids(case, query=""):
+    """Actual task-id sequence rendered by GET /tasks?<query> (title links)."""
+    r = case.request("GET", "/tasks" + query)
+    case.assertEqual(200, r.status, query)
+    return [int(m) for m in re.findall(r"/tasks/form\?id=(\d+)",
+                                       r.read().decode())]
+
+
+class QueryCase(HttpCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+
+
+class TestDefaultOrdering(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-001 (rule R-QUERY-001): no params -> every task, most-recently-
+    updated first (TaskDao.java:171): exactly [4,3,2,1] on the seed."""
+
+    def test_s_query_001_default_order_updated_desc(self):
+        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(self))
+
+
+class TestStatusFilter(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-002 (rule R-QUERY-002): status=<s> narrows to exact matches.
+    CHARACTERIZATION: a non-status token (BOGUS) is not validated; the
+    query simply matches nothing -> empty list."""
+
+    def test_s_query_002_each_status_filter(self):
+        self.assertEqual([1], listed_ids(self, "?status=OPEN"))
+        self.assertEqual([2], listed_ids(self, "?status=IN_PROGRESS"))
+        self.assertEqual([3], listed_ids(self, "?status=PENDING_APPROVAL"))
+        self.assertEqual([4], listed_ids(self, "?status=DONE"))
+
+    def test_s_query_002_unknown_status_matches_nothing(self):
+        self.assertEqual([], listed_ids(self, "?status=BOGUS"))
+
+
+class TestAssigneeFilter(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-003 (rule R-QUERY-003): assignee=<id> narrows to that person
+    (dev, id 3, owns tasks 1-3; admin, id 1, owns task 4).
+    CHARACTERIZATION: assignee= (empty) means NO filtering
+    (TaskListServlet.java:31) — there is no unassigned-only filter; and
+    every seeded task is assigned, so no unassigned row exists to select.
+    CHARACTERIZATION: non-numeric assignee -> OBSERVED 500 (unhandled
+    NumberFormatException at TaskListServlet.java:31; same robustness gap
+    shape as F-002 — reported as a finding candidate this run)."""
+
+    def test_s_query_003_assignee_filter(self):
+        self.assertEqual([3, 2, 1], listed_ids(self, "?assignee=3"))
+        self.assertEqual([4], listed_ids(self, "?assignee=1"))
+
+    def test_s_query_003_empty_assignee_means_no_filter(self):
+        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(self, "?assignee="))
+
+    def test_s_query_003_non_numeric_assignee_observed_500(self):
+        r = self.request("GET", "/tasks?assignee=abc")
+        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)
+
+
+class TestCombinedFilter(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-004 (rule R-QUERY-004): status AND assignee combine
+    (TaskDao.java:52-54 joins with AND)."""
+
+    def test_s_query_004_status_and_assignee(self):
+        self.assertEqual([1], listed_ids(self, "?status=OPEN&assignee=3"))
+        self.assertEqual([], listed_ids(self, "?status=DONE&assignee=3"))
+
+
+class TestWhitelistedSorts(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-005 (rules R-QUERY-005..R-QUERY-005): every whitelisted sort token
+    yields its exact source-defined sequence on the seed
+    (TaskDao.java:154-172): priority is semantic HIGH>MEDIUM>LOW (not
+    alphabetical); due sorts ascending with NULLS LAST (PostgreSQL ASC
+    default); title breaks ties by id."""
+
+    def test_s_query_005_every_whitelisted_sort_token(self):
+        expected = {
+            "updated": [4, 3, 2, 1],   # explicit default token
+            "status": [4, 2, 1, 3],    # alphabetical + updated DESC tiebreak
+            "priority": [2, 1, 3, 4],  # semantic HIGH,MEDIUM,LOW
+            "due": [3, 1, 2, 4],       # ASC, NULLS LAST
+            "due_date": [3, 1, 2, 4],  # alias of due
+            "title": [4, 1, 3, 2],     # alphabetical, tie -> id
+            "id": [1, 2, 3, 4],
+        }
+        for token, ids in expected.items():
+            with self.subTest(sort=token):
+                self.assertEqual(ids, listed_ids(self, "?sort=" + token))
+
+
+class TestInvalidSortFallback(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-006 (rule R-QUERY-006): unrecognized sort tokens — including
+    column-name/injection-shaped input — fall back to the default
+    updated-DESC clause (TaskDao.java:171); the whitelist never
+    concatenates the request value."""
+
+    def test_s_query_006_invalid_sorts_fall_back_to_default(self):
+        for bad in ("bogus", "t.title", "id; DROP TABLE task",
+                    "updated_at DESC--"):
+            with self.subTest(sort=bad):
+                q = "?" + urllib.parse.urlencode({"sort": bad})
+                self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(self, q))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+"""Lifecycle + bulk family (run 02, item T-LIFE): start/submit/reopen
+transitions and their state guards, not-found/unknown/malformed requests,
+and reproductions of suspected defects F-001 (bulkClose) and F-002
+(malformed id).
+
+Source basis (frozen ~/app @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c):
+- web/TaskActionServlet.java:23-74 (switch, guards, redirect)
+- web/TaskActionServlet.java:76-88 + dao/TaskDao.java:128-147 (bulkClose:
+  NO canApprove check, NO starting-state guard; SQL sets every non-DONE
+  listed id to DONE) — the guard-structure difference F-001 records.
+- web/TaskActionServlet.java:31: Long.parseLong(id) before the switch ->
+  NumberFormatException -> 500 on malformed id (F-002).
+- web/AuthFilter.java:40-47: only approve/reject are role-gated, at filter
+  level, BEFORE the servlet's 404 (ordering per R-APR-002 record).
+Approve/reject success+refusal cases live in tests/test_approval_slice.py
+(inherited, non-overlapping) and are NOT repeated here.
+Seeded tasks: 1 OPEN, 2 IN_PROGRESS, 3 PENDING_APPROVAL, 4 DONE.
+Placeholder EARS IDs (S-LIF-*/R-LIF-*) pending analyst batches.
+"""
+import subprocess
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from httpcase import HttpCase
+
+OUTPUTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+
+SEED_STATUSES = {1: "OPEN", 2: "IN_PROGRESS", 3: "PENDING_APPROVAL", 4: "DONE"}
+
+
+def reset_db():
+    subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "reset"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    )
+
+
+def oracle_statuses():
+    """Read-only oracle: {id: status} from `SELECT id,status FROM task`."""
+    out = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "sql",
+         "SELECT id,status FROM task ORDER BY id"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    ).stdout
+    return {int(line.split("|")[0]): line.split("|")[1].strip()
+            for line in out.strip().splitlines()}
+

+class LifecycleCase(HttpCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()

+
+class TestStartTransition(LifecycleCase):
+    """S-LIFE-001 (rule R-LIFE-001): start on OPEN task 1 (as dev — start has
+    NO role gate, TaskActionServlet.java:40-43) -> 302 /tasks, task 1
+    IN_PROGRESS. Start on task 2 (IN_PROGRESS) -> 409 'Cannot start a task
+    in status IN_PROGRESS (expected OPEN)', no mutation."""
+
+    def test_s_life_001_start_open_task(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(1, "start")
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/tasks", r.headers.get("Location"))
+        self.assertEqual({**SEED_STATUSES, 1: "IN_PROGRESS"}, oracle_statuses())
+
+    def test_s_life_004_start_wrong_state_conflict(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(2, "start")
+        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
+        self.assertIn("Cannot start a task in status IN_PROGRESS (expected OPEN)",
+                      r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEED_STATUSES, oracle_statuses())
+
+
+class TestSubmitTransition(LifecycleCase):
+    """S-LIFE-002 (rule R-LIFE-002): submit on IN_PROGRESS task 2 (as dev —
+    no role gate, :44-47) -> 302, task 2 PENDING_APPROVAL. Submit on OPEN
+    task 1 -> 409 '(expected IN_PROGRESS)', no mutation."""
+
+    def test_s_life_002_submit_in_progress_task(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(2, "submit")
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual({**SEED_STATUSES, 2: "PENDING_APPROVAL"},
+                         oracle_statuses())

+    def test_s_life_004_submit_wrong_state_conflict(self):

+        r = self.task_action(1, "submit")
+        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)

+                      r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEED_STATUSES, oracle_statuses())
+

+class TestReopenTransition(LifecycleCase):

+class TestNotFoundAndOrdering(LifecycleCase):

+class TestBulkCloseByMember(LifecycleCase):

+class TestBulkCloseMixedAndEmpty(LifecycleCase):
+    """S-BULK-002/S-BULK-001 (rule R-BULK-002; F-001-related characterization): bulkClose
+    ids=2,4 as dev -> 302; task 2 (IN_PROGRESS) OBSERVED -> DONE; task 4 was
+    already DONE and is untouched by the SQL (WHERE status <> 'DONE').
+    bulkClose with NO ids param -> 302 /tasks, no mutation
+    (TaskActionServlet.java:77-81)."""
+
+    def test_s_bulk_002_bulkclose_mixed_states(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/action",
+                         [("action", "bulkClose"), ("ids", "2"), ("ids", "4")])
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual({**SEED_STATUSES, 2: "DONE"}, oracle_statuses())
+
+    def test_s_bulk_001_bulkclose_without_ids_is_noop(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/action", {"action": "bulkClose"})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/tasks", r.headers.get("Location"))
+        self.assertEqual(SEED_STATUSES, oracle_statuses())

+        body = self.request("GET", "/projects").read().decode()
+        self.assertIn("Apollo", body)
+        # Re-archive of an already-ARCHIVED project: 302, no state change.
+        r = self.request("POST", "/projects", {"action": "archive", "id": "3"})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual(("3", "Legacy Archive", "ARCHIVED", "1"),

+class TestTaskCreateBlankTitleRefused(HttpCase):
+    """S-FORM-004 (rule R-FORM-003): blank/whitespace title -> 200 re-render
+    with 'Title is required' (TaskFormServlet.java:44-48); NO row persisted."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_form_004_blank_title_rerenders_without_row(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        for bad in ("", "   "):
+            r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                             {"title": bad, "projectId": "1"})
+            self.assertEqual(200, r.status, repr(bad))
+            self.assertIn("Title is required", r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEEDED_COUNT, len(oracle_tasks()))
+
+
+class TestTaskCreateInvalidProjectRefused(HttpCase):
+    """S-FORM-005 (rule R-FORM-005): missing or nonexistent projectId -> 200
+    re-render 'A task must belong to an existing project'
+    (TaskFormServlet.java:49-53); NO row persisted."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_form_005_missing_or_unknown_project_refused(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        for params in ({"title": "x"}, {"title": "x", "projectId": "999"}):
+            r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form", params)
+            self.assertEqual(200, r.status, params)
+            self.assertIn("A task must belong to an existing project",
+                          r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEEDED_COUNT, len(oracle_tasks()))
+
+
+class TestTaskNullableRoundTrip(HttpCase):
+    """S-FORM-006 (rules R-FORM-004, R-FORM-006): nullable assignee/due_date
+    round-trip — create unassigned, edit to set assignee+due date (status
+    untouched by update, TaskDao.java:97-112), edit again clearing both back
+    to NULL (empty params -> setNull, TaskFormServlet.java:60-62).
+
+    CHARACTERIZATION note (observed, no intent claim): the edit at step 2
+    posts projectId=2, but TaskDao.update()'s SQL has no project_id column,
+    so the persisted project stays 1 — the form's project field is silently
+    dropped on edit. Reported as a finding candidate this run."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_form_006_nullable_fields_round_trip(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                         {"title": "Nullables", "projectId": "1",
+                          "assigneeId": "", "dueDate": ""})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        row = oracle_tasks()[-1]
+        self.assertEqual(("5", "Nullables", "OPEN", "MEDIUM", "", "1", ""), row)
+        # Edit: assignee/due/priority persist; status stays OPEN; project_id
+        # OBSERVED unchanged (1) although projectId=2 was posted — update SQL
+        # simply has no project_id column (TaskDao.java:97-112).
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                         {"id": "5", "title": "Nullables", "projectId": "2",
+                          "assigneeId": "4", "dueDate": "2026-12-31",
+                          "priority": "LOW"})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/tasks/form?id=5&saved=1", r.headers.get("Location"))
+        self.assertEqual(("5", "Nullables", "OPEN", "LOW", "4", "1",
+                          "2026-12-31"), oracle_tasks()[-1])
+        # Clear them again: empty strings persist SQL NULLs.
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                         {"id": "5", "title": "Nullables", "projectId": "2",
+                          "assigneeId": "", "dueDate": ""})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual(("5", "Nullables", "OPEN", "MEDIUM", "", "1", ""),
+                         oracle_tasks()[-1])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()
